a:T852,No new algorithms are proposed; the core contribution is the integration of existing distributed training components (FSDP/ZeRO-3, dataset sharding, checkpoint fault tolerance, hardware telemetry) into a B300-adapted operational discipline, delivering four reusable artifacts:
1. Telemetry-based triage system: Abandons GPU utilization, uses B300-calibrated board power bands combined with three dimensions — cross-rank symmetry and duration — to rapidly classify compute (~700W+), communication (~640W, uniform power across all ranks), data starvation (~575W, scattered cross-rank power), checkpoint/deadlock (~190W; checkpoint only drops power on rank 0 and recovers within 2 minutes, deadlock shows zero power on one rank with full load on others and no recovery), and idle states. It is paired with a one-line monitoring command and decision workflow, requiring no profiler attachment to locate faults.
2. Controlled negative results: Validates that three common optimizations are ineffective at the 16-GPU 32B full fine-tuning scale: local NVMe caching brings no throughput gain over NFS (the bottleneck is tokenization and multi-tenant CPU contention, not storage media), enabling gradient checkpointing reduces throughput by 1.6x (recomputation is pure waste given B300’s sufficient memory), and corrects an earlier misdiagnosis of a power anomaly.
3. Calibrated scaling reference: Provides full-epoch throughput, GPU-hour, and memory footprint measurements for 4/8/16-GPU B300 setups.
4. Failure case and hardening: Identifies an end-of-epoch NCCL deadlock caused by packing imbalance (unequal block counts across ranks after packing; ranks that finish first enter the next collective communication wait early, triggering a permanent deadlock) — a scenario not covered by PyTorch Join or drop_last. Proposes a hardening scheme consisting of a 2.7-second preflight gate (verify all ranks have identical block counts before launch, truncate to the minimum otherwise) and an external watcher (monitor power and throughput anomalies to trigger automatic restart), and accounts for the compute cost of silent deadlocks.b:T48c,The testbed is a 32.76B-parameter dense model (Qwen3-32B) trained on 2 nodes with 16 NVIDIA B300 GPUs, using FSDP FULL_SHARD (ZeRO-3). Training config: sequence length 2048, micro-batch size 1, gradient accumulation steps 8, global batch size 128 sequences (262,144 tokens/step), ~3,854 steps per epoch, AdamW with cosine warmup, lr=1e-5, bf16 mixed precision, gradient checkpointing disabled.
Key measured data:
- Memory: Per-GPU model state is 32.8GB, total footprint including activations is 82GB, 30% of B300’s 275GB memory;
- Throughput: Disabled gradient checkpointing is 1.6x faster than enabled;
- Failure cost: An undetected packing imbalance deadlock led to 12 hours of no output, corresponding to 192 wasted GPU-hours;
- Data pipeline: Without packing, throughput collapsed from 52k tok/s to 21k tok/s under multi-tenant NFS load; packing eliminated this fluctuation (core benefit is determinism, not throughput gain);
- Scaling efficiency: Full-epoch measured GPU-hours for 4/8/16-GPU setups are provided as a budget reference for similar-scale tasks.
All conclusions are validated via controlled对照 experiments to eliminate confounding variables.c:T45e,1. First read Section 3 (Telemetry-Based Triage) carefully: focus on the power band table and the symmetry distinction logic between deadlock and checkpoint, which is the most directly reusable operational tool.
2. Next prioritize Section 5 (Packing Imbalance Deadlock Case): understand the boundaries of existing distributed tools and the hardening logic of preflight gate + watcher, a hidden problem commonly encountered in large-batch training.
3. Section 4 (Negative Results): Cross-check against your own training optimization checklist to avoid ineffective optimizations on B300 and similar large-memory accelerators.
4. Section 6 (Calibrated Scaling Reference): Use directly as a reference table for hardware selection and budget estimation.
5. Skip Section 8 (Related Work); no algorithmic innovation is presented in this report.
Deep dive directions: Extend the power triage logic to calibration for other hardware (e.g., H100, H200), or integrate with DCGM for automated fault detection; the packing imbalance problem can be extended to consistency verification workflow design for pretraining data sharding.d:T5c8,The pipeline has two stages. First, the authors estimate latent task difficulty from observed agent-task outcomes. Each trial is treated as a Bernoulli observation, forming a partially observed binary response matrix over agents and tasks. They fit a one-parameter logistic IRT model, or Rasch model, with one scalar ability parameter θ_a per agent configuration and one scalar difficulty parameter β_t per task. This is preferable to raw pass rate because difficulty is inferred relative to a population of agents on a shared scale, making it more suitable for cross-benchmark comparison.

Second, they train predictors of β_t using only pre-rollout features. These include textual task descriptions, environment and action-space descriptions, metadata, and uncertainty-related signals such as entropy that can be obtained without full interaction. A major methodological contribution is the evaluation protocol. The paper argues that response AUC can look strong even when task-level difficulty estimates are poor, so it emphasizes rank-based metrics such as Spearman correlation for judging whether the predictor actually learns difficulty ordering. The residual analysis is also important: discrepancies between predicted and observed IRT difficulty are used diagnostically. Large positive residuals often indicate hidden hardness factors such as infeasible environments or broken evaluators, while large negative residuals may reveal familiarity, templating, or shortcuts.e:T525,The empirical foundation is substantial. The main analysis subset contains 5,230 tasks from 17 benchmarks, evaluated by 497 agent configurations built from 216 models and 90 scaffolds. The full corpus includes 415,470 agent-task outcomes. Domains span coding and SWE, function calling, web navigation, computer use, terminal tasks, math, ML, cybersecurity, and general-purpose suites. Task dates range from 2010 to 2026, and each agent-task pair has about 1.2 trials on average.

The headline numbers are straightforward. Under K-fold evaluation, the combined feature set reaches Spearman ρ = 0.399, significantly better than the baseline at p < 0.001. Under out-of-distribution evaluation, it reaches ρ = 0.225. Among single feature families, entropy performs best. The paper also shows that response AUC can remain high even when task-level difficulty ranking is weak, which supports the claim that rank-based evaluation is the more faithful metric. The residual case studies are especially useful: some tasks predicted to be moderate turn out to be much harder because the environment is infeasible or the verifier is faulty, while others that look difficult in text are actually easy due to familiarity or shortcuts. That makes the predictor useful not only for scoring tasks, but also for debugging benchmarks.f:T78a,方法核心是给标准 transformer 产生的 token-level latent h_t 再叠一层 higher-level abstract latent u_t，并在两个层级上同时施加自监督预测约束。具体地，模型先用主干 G_theta 从前缀 X_{1:t} 得到 lower-level latent h_t。然后用一个 sliding-window attention 模块，在最近 k 个 lower states 上做聚合，得到 higher-level latent u_t = SWA(h_{t-k+1:t})。这个设计不是任意加一层 pooling，而是显式把上层状态绑定到一个局部时间窗口，让它表示更粗粒度、跨多个 token 的抽象动态。训练时一共学四个映射：标准输出头 p_theta；lower dynamics p_psi，用于 NextLat 风格的一步 latent transition consistency；upper dynamics p_phi，用于在 abstract latent 上做 k 步前瞻预测；combined head p_rho，用 lower 与 upper latent 联合做 next-token prediction。总损失由五部分组成：标准 NTP loss、lower-level transition consistency loss、一个 KL 项、higher-level transition consistency loss、以及 combined NTP loss。论文里一个很细但重要的实现点是 stop-gradient 的放置：在 combined NTP 路径里，higher-level latent 由带 stop-grad 的 lower states 经过 SWA 得到。这样 combined head 的交叉熵只训练 SWA 和上层表示，不会反向改写 h_t。作者的理由很充分：如果不切断这条梯度，token-level 表示 h_t 会同时受到标准 NTP、lower transition consistency、以及经 SWA 回流的 combined-head 监督，形成重复计数和相互竞争的优化信号。这个处理说明作者不是把多头损失机械相加，而是在认真控制不同目标对表示空间的职责分工。另一个关键点是推理阶段只保留标准 NTP head，整个层级 apparatus 都是训练时辅助项，因此没有额外 inference overhead；如果愿意用速度换精度，也可以考虑 combined NTP，但当前实现默认不用。10:T5e6,HiLP starts from a standard transformer backbone that produces lower-level token latents h_t = G_theta(X_{1:t}). It then builds a higher-level abstract latent u_t by applying a sliding-window attention module over the most recent k lower-level states, u_t = SWA(h_{t-k+1:t}). This upper latent is meant to capture a coarser temporal abstraction over multiple tokens. The model jointly learns four parameterized components: the standard token output head p_theta, a lower-level dynamics model p_psi for NextLat-style transition consistency, an upper-level dynamics model p_phi that predicts k steps ahead in the abstract latent space, and a combined head p_rho that predicts the next token from both lower and upper latents. Training uses a weighted sum of five losses: standard NTP, lower-level latent transition consistency, a KL term, higher-level transition consistency, and a combined NTP loss. A subtle but important implementation detail is the stop-gradient placement. In the combined-NTP path, the upper latent is computed from stop-grad lower states before SWA. This prevents the combined-head cross-entropy from backpropagating into h_t through the SWA window. The authors explicitly argue that otherwise the lower token-level representation would receive overlapping and competing supervision from standard NTP, lower-level transition consistency, and the combined head. At inference time, only the standard NTP head is used, so the hierarchical apparatus acts purely as a training-time regularizer.11:T4f3,The experimental setup is reasonably substantial for a method paper: 1B-parameter models pretrained on 100B tokens using 8 NVIDIA B200 GPUs. Evaluation has two parts. First, zero-shot downstream performance is measured with LM Evaluation Harness on HumanEval and with DataComp for LLMs on symbolic and multi-step reasoning benchmarks. HiLP is reported to outperform the relevant baselines, including MTP and NextLat, especially on tasks that require longer-horizon reasoning or planning. Second, the paper evaluates speculative decoding on held-out code and Nemotron-Climbmix validation sets, reporting average accepted tokens per drafting step and draft–verifier argmax agreement for K=1..4. HiLP improves these metrics as well, suggesting that the learned latent dynamics are more rollout-stable and more useful for multi-token drafting. The most informative analysis is the latent prediction cross-entropy over rollout horizons up to 8 steps. HiLP preserves near-term prediction quality comparable to NextLat while reducing error at longer horizons, which directly supports the claim that hierarchical abstraction mitigates multi-step latent error accumulation. The excerpt does not include the full numeric tables, so exact gains cannot be quoted reliably here.12:T5a7,The main experiments use LLaDA-8B-Instruct. On GSM8K, pure decoding produces answer-only collapse on more than one third of problems with a 256-token canvas, and up to about 90% with a 512-token canvas. The final answer is typically committed in the first quarter of the denoising trajectory, while the median reasoning token is committed near the halfway point. A fixed 2×2 design crosses prompt type, reasoning requested versus answer-only, with decoder type, pure versus semi-AR. On 250 paired problems, the prompt–decoder interaction is about 35 percentage points with permutation p<1e-4. In the answer-only condition, the two decoders are statistically close. When CoT is requested, semi-AR gains about 31 points, while pure decoding gains only a small amount. The same pattern is replicated on Dream-7B and MATH-500; on MATH-500, collapse is smaller, but the order channel remains. Window restriction recovers most of the gap: all w≤64 improve pure decoding from 0.528 to roughly 0.82–0.85, close to semi-AR. A sliding window of 32 matches a block size of 32 within about one point, indicating that restricted reachability, rather than block structure itself, is the active mechanism. The paper also measures the cost: with full refinement, w=1 is best; at around eight committed tokens per step, the constraint can become harmful. In the useful regime, the gated decoder keeps near-AR accuracy with a reported 4× wall-clock speedup.13:T429,Its value for foundation-model research is diagnostic rather than merely competitive. First, it separates local perception from global spatial integration, which many existing video benchmarks do not. That matters for embodied pretraining, world modeling, and native multimodal training. Second, it shows that failure modes differ by model family: strong proprietary models improve sharply when the task is reduced to local perception, suggesting that their main weakness is cross-frame spatial integration; many open models do not improve reliably even then, indicating deficits in both perception and reasoning. Third, the paper goes beyond evaluation by introducing GST-Train and showing that targeted training data can substantially close the gap. Qwen3-VL-8B improves from 25.89 to 53.52 after fine-tuning, surpassing all zero-shot proprietary models in their setup. That is a useful signal for pretraining researchers: global spatial competence is not something to assume will emerge automatically from scale alone; data construction and task pressure matter.14:T5fc,The contribution is centered on benchmark and data design rather than a new VLM architecture. The pipeline is built on OmniGibson with BEHAVIOR-1K scenes. For each of 50 indoor environments, the system generates diverse egocentric camera trajectories and additional query viewpoints outside the trajectory. It records 3D coordinates of objects and viewpoints, projects them onto top-down maps, and automatically creates QA pairs. A key filtering rule removes any sample where the target object is visible from the query viewpoint, ensuring that object-localization questions cannot be solved from a single frame.

The task taxonomy is organized around three embodied questions: Where am I? Where is the target? What does the scene look like? These are instantiated as 12 subtasks covering self-localization, object localization, and scene-structure understanding. The top-down image is not cosmetic; it acts as an explicit probe of whether the model can map long-horizon egocentric evidence into a global scene representation.

A particularly strong part of the design is the controlled local variant. By making the target visible from the query viewpoint, the authors convert the task into one that can be handled largely by local perception. This creates a clean diagnostic split between failures caused by weak single-image understanding and failures caused by missing cross-view spatial integration. That makes GST-Bench more than a leaderboard asset; it becomes a tool for identifying what kind of spatial competence is absent.15:T584,The main evaluation covers 22 VLMs, including open-source, proprietary, and embodied-oriented models. The strongest zero-shot model, Gemini-3-Pro, reaches 42.68, far below the human score of 79.08. Open models are substantially weaker; the best reported open model, InternVL3.5-38B, stays around the low-30 range, and many models are close to random guessing. This gap is large enough that it is hard to dismiss as benchmark noise.

The local-variant analysis is one of the most informative results. Proprietary models can gain up to 39 points when the task is reduced to local perception, which strongly suggests that they already have decent local visual understanding but fail to maintain and use a global spatial state across frames. Open models, in contrast, do not improve consistently even in the local setting, pointing to a compound bottleneck involving both perception and reasoning.

The training-data experiment is equally important. Using the same generation pipeline, the authors build GST-Train from BEHAVIOR-1K, ArtVIP, HyperSim, and internal simulation scenes. Fine-tuning Qwen3-VL-8B on GST-Train raises its GST-Bench score from 25.89 to 53.52, a gain of 27.63 points, and pushes it above all proprietary models under zero-shot evaluation. That result suggests the benchmark is learnable with targeted supervision and that synthetic spatial data can materially improve global spatial reasoning.16:T471,Three components are described in the provided text:
1) SearchAuditBench: 1,243 failed trajectories collected from 8 open-weight models run under a unified scaffold (two tools: search, visit) on 5 deep-search benchmarks. Each trajectory is expert-annotated with (i) the critical error step plus a tolerance span, (ii) a 6-way search-specific root-cause taxonomy, and (iii) a reference repair directive paired with atomic grading rubrics. Only “trace-auditable” failures are kept—annotators must be able to justify the critical error using evidence inside the frozen trace.
2) Benchmarking auditors: evaluate LLMs on localization, attribution, and repair, highlighting challenges from scale/noise, lack of verifiable structure, and silent error propagation.
3) SearchAuditor method: run three complementary audit branches in parallel, adjudicate their reports over a compact evidence-grounded view of the trajectory, then synthesize process-level repair directives.

The excerpt does not specify what the three branches are or how the compact view is constructed; those are method-critical details that require the missing sections.17:T40c,Visual tool-use is formulated as a structural causal graph that separates three paths to the final answer: the direct path from raw input, observation-mediated paths (where visual content of tool outputs genuinely affects the answer), and action-induced shortcuts (where only the text of tool calls influences the answer). Three intervention protocols with increasing granularity are designed: 1) Policy-level: compare overall performance between tool-use and direct inference; 2) Trajectory-level: corrupt all returned observations at runtime and measure answer changes; 3) Step-level: replace a single observation with a counterfactual crop and compute Visual Evidence Gain (corresponding to Natural Indirect Effect) to judge whether the visual content truly alters answer preference. Based on intervention results, each rollout is categorized into four groups: Calibrated, Calling Without Looking (CWL), Looking Without Planning (LWP), and No-call; policy-level accuracy improvements are then decomposed into per-group contributions.18:T83e,方法核心是把 AIVAT 和 confidence sequences 结合成 AV-AIVAT。AIVAT 原本用于不完全信息博弈中的方差缩减，它利用 value function 对某些决策节点构造条件均值为零的校正项，从而在不引入偏差的前提下降低 payoff 方差。问题在于，经典 AIVAT 默认 value function 是冻结的，而实际评测中人们往往希望边评边学，让 value model 越来越准。论文提出的关键接口是 predictable / pre-action conditional-kernel interface：在每个 correction node，评测器必须在动作发生前就知道该动作在当下信息条件下的 conditional kernel，而且是否启用该 correction 也必须在动作前就固定。与此同时，时刻 t 的 value function 只能使用 1 到 t-1 局的数据，不能用当前局。这样可以保证当前样本不会“选择自己的校正项”，从而保住 conditional mean-zero 的性质。

在这个接口之上，论文给了两类 confidence sequence。第一类是 EB-CS，强调 exact finite-sample validity，但要求事先给出一个与数据无关、几乎处处成立的 corrected payoff 上界 B。作者在 Leduc 上推导并穷举验证了这样的 bound。第二类是 AsympCS，放宽为渐近有效，需要满足极限方差条件，实际中更高效。论文还给出一个理论结果：在 variance-adaptive width 和 sublinear variance-regret 条件下，past-only online learner 的停止效率在渐近上不会比 deterministic oracle-width benchmark 慢。直观上，这说明在线学 value model 只要不偷看当前局，就可以逐步逼近“知道最好 value function”的评测效率。

另一个很有价值的分析是 width floor。作者指出，方差缩减并不总会等比例转化为更早停止，因为在 bet-capped EB-CS 下，声明的 payoff bound 会引入一个确定性的宽度下界 4B log(2/α)/t。一旦这个 floor 开始主导，继续降低方差也不会明显缩短停止时间。论文把这件事整理成三阶段的 order-level design guide，这比单纯报告“方差降了多少倍”更有解释力。19:T866,The method combines AIVAT with confidence sequences to form AV-AIVAT. AIVAT is a variance-reduction estimator for imperfect-information games. It uses a value function to construct conditional mean-zero corrections at selected decision points, reducing payoff variance without introducing bias. Classical AIVAT assumes a frozen value function, but in practice one would like the value model to improve during evaluation. The key contribution is a predictable, pre-action conditional-kernel interface. At each correction node, the evaluator must know the action’s conditional kernel given the information available immediately before the action, and the decision to enable the correction must also be fixed before the action is observed. In addition, the value function at time t may use only games 1 through t-1, never game t itself. These conditions prevent the current sample from choosing its own correction and preserve the conditional mean-zero property.

On top of this interface, the paper develops two confidence-sequence options. EB-CS provides exact finite-sample validity, but it requires a data-independent almost-sure bound on the corrected payoff. The authors derive and exhaustively verify such a bound in Leduc. AsympCS instead relies on asymptotic variance conditions and is more efficient in practice. The theory also includes a no-asymptotic-delay style result: under variance-adaptive widths and sublinear variance regret, a past-only online value learner asymptotically matches a deterministic oracle-width benchmark. Intuitively, online value learning can approach oracle stopping efficiency as long as it does not peek at the current game.

A particularly useful part of the paper is the width-floor analysis. Variance reduction does not automatically translate into proportionally earlier stopping. In the bet-capped EB-CS setting, the declared payoff bound induces a deterministic lower bound on interval width, 4B log(2/alpha) / t. Once this floor dominates, further variance reduction has little effect on stopping time. The resulting three-regime design guide is more informative than reporting variance reduction alone.1a:T688,The main empirical study uses a heads-up no-limit hold’em corpus with 71,439 paired hands across 15 LLM-agent configurations. AIVAT reduces variance by a median factor of 54 relative to raw per-hand payoffs. The more important metric is stopping time. At alpha = 0.05 and a target half-width of one big blind, paired replay shows a median raw-to-AIVAT stopping-time ratio of 74 under AsympCS. In practical terms, the same evaluation precision can be reached with roughly one seventy-fourth as many hands. Under the descriptive EB-CS execution, the ratio is only 1.37. The paper explains this large gap through the width floor: once the deterministic bound-driven floor dominates, lower variance no longer buys much earlier stopping.

For online learning, the authors test past-only value updates under two controlled profile-mismatch settings. The online learner recovers 77% to 79% of the frozen-to-oracle variance gap, which supports the claim that online improvement is possible without sacrificing validity.

The rolling-leaderboard simulation is also important. Under the null, continuously monitoring an ordinary fixed-sample interval and stopping at the first favorable look yields a 61% false-positive rate. Recomputing a confidence sequence at that same stopping time overturns essentially all such claims. When AsympCS continues under its own stopping rule, it detects injected effects of 0.1 and 0.2 big blinds in 81% and 100% of entries by 5,000 hands. The paper also specifies what must be released for reproducibility: corrected outcomes up to the stopping time, the stopping rule, the target, the confidence level, the declared bound, and the CS settings.1b:T4f4,For a quick read, focus on three things. First, the motivation for why ordinary optional stopping fails and why AIVAT and confidence sequences solve complementary parts of the problem. Second, the definition of the pre-action conditional-kernel interface, which is the central validity condition of the paper. Third, the width-floor analysis and the HUNL results, because they explain when variance reduction does and does not convert into cheaper evaluation.

A good reading order is: problem setup and motivation, the AV-AIVAT interface, the distinction between EB-CS and AsympCS, the HUNL experiments, and the rolling-leaderboard simulation. The proof details can be skimmed at first, but it is worth understanding what Proposition 1, Proposition 2, and Theorem 2 each guarantee. Around 40 to 60 minutes should be enough to extract the main ideas and practical implications.

Two caveats are worth keeping in mind. The method assumes access to the action conditional kernel, which may be hard to obtain in some real agent environments. And AsympCS is more efficient but does not provide the same exact finite-sample certificate as EB-CS. Which variant is appropriate depends on whether strict certification or sample efficiency matters more in the target benchmark.1c:T4a5,Proposes PaDoc, a layout-grounded parallel document parser built on a region-sufficiency assumption: conditioned on the full page image and a region's layout specification, content generation for that region is independent of content from other regions. The joint parsing distribution is factorized into a serial layout prediction stream, and parallel content generation branches spawned immediately after each layout is predicted, reducing logical decoding depth from the sum of all region content lengths to the longest single layout-content path. During training, an ancestor attention mask restricts each content branch to attend only to the shared page image and historical layout prefix, with no access to sibling region content. This is implemented with packed variable-length sequences, requiring no auxiliary prediction heads or extra losses, and retaining the standard next-token prediction objective. During inference, PaDoc runs on vLLM with global shared KV cache for the page image and layout prefix, decoding each region content branch as a concurrent request. The model is initialized from Qwen3-VL-2B, adapted via continual pre-training followed by supervised fine-tuning.1d:T7d7,KVAE is a family of encoder-decoder tokenizers for multimodal generative modeling. The visual side includes two causal video tokenizers: KVAE-3D with 4x8x8 compression and 16 latent channels, and KVAE-3D with 4x16x16 compression and 64 channels. The image tokenizer, KVAE-2D, uses 8x spatial compression with 32 channels. The audio tokenizer is continuous, full-band 48 kHz, with a 50 Hz latent stream and 64 channels.

The most interesting design choices appear in the video tokenizer. First, the backbone is attention-free and built around Conv3D blocks. The motivation is efficiency and length generalization: convolutional tokenizers are cheaper to train and can process arbitrarily long sequences at inference through caching, which is useful for long videos and causal generation settings. Second, the tokenizer is causal, implemented with asymmetric temporal padding, so it can support both images and videos without leaking future frames. Third, temporal and spatial downsampling/upsampling are applied sequentially rather than collapsed into a single operation. The paper contrasts this with approaches that patchify aggressively in early layers; KVAE-4x16x16 delays patching to the downsampling blocks instead. Fourth, the models use spatial RMSNorm, which allows segment-size adjustment at inference. Fifth, the encoder and decoder are intentionally asymmetric, especially in the higher-compression model where the decoder is made substantially wider. This reflects a clear hypothesis: under strong compression, it is better to keep the encoder stable and efficient while giving the decoder more capacity to reconstruct detail.

Another important point is that the paper treats diffusability as a primary tokenizer property. Reconstruction metrics alone are not enough. A tokenizer can reconstruct well yet still produce a latent space that is awkward for diffusion training. The later sections on diffusability evaluation and design ablations are therefore central to the paper’s contribution.1e:T6c7,The reported evaluation spans reconstruction, downstream generation quality, diffusability analysis, and architecture ablations. On the visual side, KVAE-3D is compared against open-source video tokenizers on both reconstruction and generation, using metrics such as PSNR, LPIPS, and Fréchet-style generation scores. KVAE-2D is evaluated similarly for images. On the audio side, the paper reports objective metrics such as PESQ and emphasizes the practical operating point of full-band 48 kHz audio with a 50 Hz latent stream and 64 channels.

The abstract claims that KVAE matches or exceeds open-source alternatives on both reconstruction and generation. However, the provided full-text excerpt does not include the complete result tables, so it is not possible to restate all dataset-specific numbers reliably from the available material alone. Even so, the experimental structure already conveys several useful points. The tokenizer is evaluated not only as an autoencoder but also as part of the full generation pipeline, which is the right criterion. The video tokenizer is designed for long-sequence and causal settings, so the relevant comparison is not just short-clip reconstruction. And the paper explicitly includes diffusability evaluation and design ablations, which suggests the authors are trying to explain why the latent space is easier to model, not merely that it reconstructs better.

When reading the full paper, the most important quantitative checks are likely to be: the trade-off between the 4x16x16(64ch) and 4x8x8(16ch) variants in token cost versus generation quality; the contribution of the wider decoder; and whether the attention-free design preserves enough semantic structure under high compression.1f:T514,Read it in four passes: problem framing, architecture, end-to-end validation, and ablations. Start with the introduction to see how the authors position tokenizers as part of the generative model rather than a detachable front-end. Then focus on the video-tokenizer method section. The key design decisions are the attention-free Conv3D backbone, causal setup, spatial RMSNorm, sequential temporal/spatial downsampling, and encoder-decoder asymmetry. The useful question to keep in mind is why these choices are preferable to heavier spatiotemporal attention or more aggressive early patchification.

After that, inspect the reconstruction and generation tables together. Do not read PSNR or LPIPS in isolation; check whether a tokenizer that reconstructs well also improves downstream generation. Then read the diffusability section and the ablations carefully. That is where the paper either becomes a set of engineering choices or turns into a more general tokenizer-design lesson. If your interest is unified multimodal pretraining, the audio section is worth reading as well because it extends similar design principles to high-rate continuous audio. A first pass should take around 40 minutes, and a reproduction-oriented read will require careful attention to the method and ablation sections.20:T521,From the available full-text excerpt (intro only), the approach is organized around three coupled issues and corresponding design choices:
1) Unified formulation: treat generation, reference-based synthesis, temporal extension, and editing—now extended to joint audio-video—as one conditional denoising family with varying condition/target configurations.
2) Explicit token roles: instead of implicit concatenation, attach role information to tokens so the model is told which segments are reconstruction targets, which are to be preserved, and which are guidance-only references.
3) Conditioning duality: implement both a semantic pathway (instruction following, reasoning over text + visual evidence) and a latent pathway (high-fidelity preservation of identity/texture/layout/motion). In audio-video, this must also handle modality absence and asymmetric roles per modality.
4) Data consistency & balance: emphasize truly aligned audio-video pairs and a task mixture that does not let data-rich objectives dominate.

However, the excerpt stops before the method section; details such as the exact role representation, backbone topology (single-stream vs dual-tower), fusion mechanism, loss weighting, and sampling of condition-output configurations cannot be stated faithfully without the missing sections.21:T5eb,Two pillars.
(1) MIST-1000 benchmark: each item is instantiated into four matched variants where the question, answer space/type, gold answer, and a plausible wrong answer are held fixed; only the surrounding context changes: clean (no added context), misleading (context nudges toward the plausible wrong answer), correct-context (context supports the gold), and irrelevant (natural related text that does not contain the answer). The dataset contains 1,000 items (800 adapted from existing QA/math/reasoning benchmarks, 200 newly written longer scenarios) with a two-pass human QA process to control ambiguity, leakage, and plausibility.
(2) Metrics + training:
- SC2W (signal-induced correct-to-wrong): computed on the subset of items the model answers correctly in the clean condition, measuring how often misleading context flips them to wrong—separating susceptibility from base competence.
- SCOPE (Selective Context Preference Optimization): a signal-counterfactual preference framework. It mines the critical failures from a base model: cases where the model is correct in clean but wrong under misleading context. For each, it forms a preference pair (truth-consistent response preferred over signal-following response). The key is not changing DPO’s objective, but balancing the training mixture: reuse the same preference pair across all four context conditions and weight misleading pairs equally against clean/correct/irrelevant controls, preventing the “learn to ignore all context” shortcut.22:T439,The proposed method is Ranking-based Reward Construction. It uses a generative reward model only for relative preference ranking, then derives scalar rewards from the resulting order. The first mechanism is self-competitive ranking: for a given prompt, multiple responses sampled from the current policy are compared against each other, and their relative order is mapped into rewards. This aligns naturally with group-relative policy optimization. The second mechanism is anchor-guided ranking: a small set of anchor responses serves as reference points, and candidate responses are compared against these anchors. This reduces the reward-model call pattern for larger candidate sets from a sorting-style O(m log m) regime to O(m n), where m is the number of sampled responses and n is the number of anchors. When n is small, this is more scalable. Unlike GenRM-style preference-token probabilities, RRC does not rely on a single token probability. Unlike approaches that suppress reasoning and emit only labels, it can preserve the generative judge’s textual reasoning process.23:T4a6,HERALD treats a reward as a program and subjects it to structured counterfactual unit tests on the same question rather than evaluating a trained policy by aggregate score. First, it constructs minimal same-question trajectory interventions: same-final deletes all search actions while preserving the answer and citations; fake-ID replaces a citation with a nonexistent corpus ID; adaptive laundering replaces it with a real passage that the agent never retrieved. Second, it draws a strict information boundary: attack generation is label-free and accesses only the question, logged trajectory, and corpus, never gold answers or support annotations; if reward computation uses annotated signals, that selection is reported as an oracle upper bound rather than a candidate-visible attack. Third, it enumerates the full 2^3 lattice over three detectors—retrieval-use, membership in retrieved evidence, and fake IDs—and reports paired reward margins to identify an inclusion-minimal repair instead of claiming victory from a large detector bundle. The key detector L is an exact membership check that fires when a cited passage exists in the corpus but was absent from retrieved evidence.24:T465,The audit is run on four Qwen3-8B trajectory pools from HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue, covering 3,829 isolated trajectories. The base reward R0 already rejects same-final search deletion and fake citation IDs, but a label-free adaptive citation-laundering attack succeeds: replacing a citation with a real but unretrieved corpus passage can still achieve high reward. The full 2^3 ablation shows that strengthening L alone is the observed minimal repair on these pools, while the other two exact checks are redundant. With L added, R[L] reports zero empirical attack success rate at roughly 0.50% one-sided cost on the relevant metric, while a broader strict reward remains attackable because its oracle support-ID penalty can be removed or avoided. A matched 5M-token Search-GRPO comparison shows that only 18 of 58,368 training trajectories trigger the detector, so the training signal is extremely sparse; on 256 paired questions per benchmark, R[L] improves targeted attackability and some evidence-quality measures but does not guarantee lower natural violation incidence or uniform exact-match non-inferiority.25:T472,The method follows the OPD / OPD2 line. In OPD, the student first samples a rollout from its current policy. That sampled trajectory is then scored token by token by the teacher, and the student is updated to reduce the discrepancy on the sampled tokens. Compared with sequence-level RL, this gives denser token-level supervision while preserving the on-policy nature. OPD2 changes the reward definition: instead of using log p_teacher minus log p_student, it uses log p_teacher minus log p_teacher_base. The motivation is to isolate capabilities acquired during post-training, especially reasoning, while suppressing generic language preferences and stylistic biases already present in the teacher’s base model. The paper also keeps the two stabilizers introduced in prior OPD2 work: reward centering to form an advantage-like signal, and a conditioning criterion based on the original OPD signal to avoid pathological convergence. Empirically, the study builds a 100K multilingual math training set in English, Korean, and Japanese, and compares multilingual OPD and OPD2 as well as English-only OPD on Qwen3 instruction-tuned models.26:T6a0,The method edits a single weight matrix W at a time, either the LM head or the down-projection in an MLP block. The model performs one forward pass on forget data and one on retain data, collects the input activations to W, and stacks them as key matrices. Keys are extracted only at positions where the next token should be forgotten or preserved, so the optimization is token-level rather than sample-level.

The objective is a ridge-regularized least-squares problem over an additive update ΔW. One term pushes the edited matrix toward a predefined forgetting target on forget keys. Another term constrains outputs on retain keys to stay close to the original model. Because the objective is quadratic and convex in ΔW, the optimum has a closed-form solution. This is the main departure from methods such as gradient ascent, RKLD, NPO, or preference-style unlearning, which search for a compromise through SGD.

The paper uses two target designs. Token suppression directly lowers the model’s tendency to emit the memorized answer token or related logits. Representation corruption instead perturbs intermediate representations so downstream layers cannot easily reconstruct the deleted content. The authors also introduce a logit-lens-based layer selection strategy: they inspect which intermediate layers already make the target content linearly decodable through the LM head, then edit only those high-impact layers. This avoids broad model changes. A notable extra is exact influence auditing. Because the update is analytic, the contribution of an individual deletion request to the final parameter edit can be decomposed exactly, which is much harder with iterative gradient methods.27:T66d,The paper evaluates forgetting-utility trade-offs on five benchmarks and uses gold retraining as a reference point. Three findings matter most. First, efficiency: GROM needs only forward-pass statistics for forget and retain sets plus a closed-form solve per edited layer, leading to second-level execution and up to two orders of magnitude speedup over gradient-based baselines. Second, effectiveness: on Pareto trade-offs, it is typically better than or competitive with GA, GradDiff, RMU, RKLD, NPO, and SimNPO, meaning stronger forgetting at matched utility or better utility at matched forgetting. Third, robustness: under the recent low-bit quantization recovery attack, a strong gradient-based baseline regains a substantial amount of supposedly erased content, while GROM remains close to the retrained model in forgetfulness.

The most informative ablations are not just average scores but the structure of the edit: which layers are edited, whether the LM head or MLP down-projection is modified, token suppression versus representation corruption, the ridge coefficient, the size of the retain set, and interference across multiple deletion requests. The broader experimental message is that local linear edits can handle a meaningful fraction of memory-style unlearning when layer selection is accurate and retain constraints are strong, but distributed multi-layer knowledge remains a harder case. The provided full-text excerpt does not include all numeric tables, so exact benchmark-by-benchmark scores cannot be reproduced here, but the speedup scale, Pareto behavior, and quantization robustness are the key empirical takeaways.28:T782,实验最有信息量的部分有三块。第一块是校准本身是否必要。论文指出，进入 contrastive calibration 的候选任务都已经通过结构验证和 self-solving，但初始时只有 19% 满足“强通过、弱失败”的目标关系；经过多轮 revision 和 re-probing，累计接受率提升到 96%。这说明 executable validity 与 solver-relative learnability 之间有明显缺口，而且这个缺口可以通过行为驱动的重写显著缩小。

第二块是不同数据构造策略对下游 agent 训练的影响。作者构建了 5,431 个校准后的 terminal tasks，并比较了几种训练数据来源：仅 authoring+validation、单 solver 反馈、多 solver calibration、contrastive calibration。Terminal-Bench 2.0 上，authoring+validation 只有 22.47%，单 solver 反馈 24.34%，multi-solver calibration 提升到 29.21%，contrastive calibration 31.09%。这里的提升不是边际噪声，而是相对清晰地说明：只知道任务“能做”不够，知道“谁能做、谁做不好”更有训练价值。

第三块是用完整校准数据训练模型后的结果。CalibForge-30B-A3B 和 CalibForge-35B-A3B 在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上分别达到 32.58% 和 47.57%，比最强 baseline 高 6.36 和 6.75 个百分点。更关键的是泛化：35B 模型在 SWE-bench Pro 上 44.32%，在 Doc2Repo 上 48.77%，都是同 backbone 下所比较训练数据源里最好的结果。这个结果支持一个判断：校准得到的不是只对合成任务有效的“刷榜数据”，而是更接近真实软件工程交互中有效 supervision 的数据分布。

从消融角度，最值得注意的是 single-solver feedback 与 multi/contrastive calibration 的差距。它说明单一 solver 的成功或失败信号太粗，容易把任务推向该 solver 的局部偏好，而多 solver 分歧或强弱对比能提供更稳定的难度定位信号。29:T8e4,CalibForge starts from a clue and builds a terminal task automatically, but the real novelty is the adversarial solver calibration stage after initial authoring. The pipeline has four main parts. First, an authoring agent works in a draft sandbox with web search, shell execution, and file editing. It researches concrete engineering issues from documentation, GitHub repositories and issues, and Stack Overflow, looking for reproducible problems such as dependency conflicts, version-specific bugs, configuration pitfalls, and edge cases. Second, it jointly writes the task instruction, execution environment, and verification tests, then performs structural validation and self-solving so the candidate is executable, checkable, and solvable by the system itself.

Third, the candidate enters calibration. The paper defines two retention criteria. Multi-solver calibration seeks disagreement in a heterogeneous solver pool: at least one solver must pass and at least one must fail. Contrastive calibration is stricter: a designated stronger solver must pass while a designated weaker solver must fail. In both cases, learnability is defined relationally, not by an absolute difficulty score. Fourth, if the criterion is not met, the authoring agent revises the task and revalidates it before another probing round. Importantly, revision is not limited to wording tweaks. The agent may revisit external research, change the technical direction, rewrite the environment, modify tests, or alter constraints.

Two design choices matter. First, the feedback includes not only verified pass/fail outcomes but also full trajectories. That gives the authoring agent actionable evidence about why a task failed or succeeded, which can be used to reduce noise, sharpen constraints, or introduce steps that better separate strong and weak solvers. Second, the loop is adversarial but constrained: retention always requires at least one real successful solve, so the system does not drift toward pathological or unsolvable tasks. Compared with one-shot authoring plus validation, or with single-solver scoring, this adds a behavior-driven revision loop. That is the key difference, because learnability is treated as something that can be actively shaped rather than passively measured.2a:T76d,The most informative results come in three layers. First, calibration is clearly necessary. All candidates entering contrastive calibration have already passed structural validation and self-solving, yet only 19% initially satisfy the target strong-pass/weak-fail relation. After iterative revision and re-probing, cumulative acceptance rises to 96%. This shows that executable validity and solver-relative learnability are far from equivalent, and that the gap can be closed by behavior-guided rewriting.

Second, the paper compares training data produced by different construction strategies. On Terminal-Bench 2.0, authoring plus validation alone yields 22.47% accuracy. Adding single-solver feedback gives 24.34%. Multi-solver calibration reaches 29.21%, and contrastive calibration reaches 31.09%. The gain is large enough to support a clear interpretation: knowing that a task is solvable is not enough; knowing which solvers succeed and which fail provides substantially better supervision.

Third, the authors train on the full set of 5,431 calibrated tasks. CalibForge-30B-A3B and CalibForge-35B-A3B obtain 32.58% and 47.57% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, outperforming the strongest baselines by 6.36 and 6.75 points. The transfer results matter even more. The 35B model reaches 44.32% on SWE-bench Pro and 48.77% on Doc2Repo, the best among the compared training-data sources for the same backbone. That suggests the calibrated tasks are not merely overfit synthetic exercises; they appear to induce supervision that transfers to broader software-engineering settings.

The ablation that stands out is the gap between single-solver feedback and multi-solver or contrastive calibration. A single solver provides a coarse signal and can bias task construction toward that solver’s idiosyncrasies. Solver disagreement and strong-vs-weak contrast provide a more stable handle on difficulty placement.2b:T5ce,If time is limited, start with the Introduction sections that define the solver-relative learnable zone, then read the methodology around Figure 2 and Algorithm 1 to understand the control flow of the author–solver loop. After that, focus on the two calibration criteria, multi-solver and contrastive. Those sections contain the real contribution. Without them, the system would be just another task-generation pipeline.

In the experiments, prioritize three result blocks. First, the fraction of initially authored tasks that already satisfy the contrastive relation versus the cumulative acceptance after revision rounds. Second, the comparison among authoring-only, single-solver feedback, multi-solver calibration, and contrastive calibration on Terminal-Bench 2.0. Third, the final 30B and 35B training results and their transfer to SWE-bench Pro and Doc2Repo. Those are the results that tell you whether the method changes supervision quality rather than just producing more tasks.

Three deeper questions are worth following while reading. How exactly are trajectories converted into revision actions? How sensitive is the learnable zone to the composition of the solver pool? And can this idea generalize beyond terminal tasks to code completion, tool-use, web agents, or even difficulty-aware sampling in broader pretraining settings? Reliable estimates would therefore allow environment designers to calibrate evaluation benchmarks and construct progressive training curricula. This becomes increasingly important as agents move into long-horizon domains, where empirical trial-and-error is a severe computational bottlene","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-06T09:33:09Z","category_id":"scaling","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05797.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是一个评测与训练管线里的前置问题：在不做 rollouts、不运行完整 agent 交互的前提下，仅根据任务描述和少量静态元信息，能否预估一个 agent 任务有多难。作者关心的不是单个模型的 uncertainty 校准，而是跨 benchmark、跨 agent 配置地定义“任务难度”这一潜变量，并检验这种 ex ante 预测能否替代昂贵的实测试跑。问题之所以重要，是因为长时程 agent benchmark 的验证成本越来越高，很多任务一次评测就要消耗大量环境交互和模型调用；如果难度不能预估，benchmark 构造、课程学习和数据筛选都会变得很低效。","importance":"对预训练研究的关联不是直接改进 base model，而是提供了一个和数据课程、评测设计、合成任务筛选都相关的工具视角。第一，它把 task difficulty 从“经验上觉得难”变成了可估计、可排序的量，这对构造 curriculum、挑选训练样本、控制 benchmark 覆盖度有现实意义。第二，论文提醒人们不要用 response-level AUC 这类看起来很高的指标误判难度预测能力，因为它可能只是在区分成功/失败样本，而没有真正学到 task-level 难度排序。第三，作者公开了一个跨 17 个 agent benchmark 的结果库，并用 IRT 建 difficulty target，这对后续做 agent scaling、benchmark contamination 分析、任务去重和难度分层采样都有参考价值。它更像一篇评测方法与数据资产论文，而不是预训练算法论文。","technical_approach":"方法分两步。第一步，从已有 agent-task 成败记录中估计每个任务的 latent difficulty。作者把每次 trial 视作 Bernoulli 观测，构造一个部分观测的 agent×task 二值响应矩阵，然后用 1PL IRT（Rasch model）拟合每个 agent 的能力参数 θ_a 和每个任务的难度参数 β_t。这样定义难度有一个好处：它不是某个单一模型的通过率，而是在共享标尺上相对于多种 agent 能力共同估出来的，因此更适合跨 benchmark 比较。\n\n第二步，用 rollout 前可获得的特征去预测 β_t。特征包括任务文本描述、环境与动作空间描述、元数据，以及作者特别强调的 entropy 等不需要完整交互即可得到的信号。论文还系统讨论了评估协议：如果用 response AUC，模型可能在样本级别上看起来预测得不错，但 task-level rank correlation 仍然很弱，因此作者主张用 Spearman ρ 这类排序指标来评估“是否真的学到了难度”。此外，论文做了 residual diagnostics：把预测难度与 IRT 估计难度的残差拿出来分析。正残差大的任务，往往是比文本表面看起来更难，原因可能是环境不可行、verifier 有问题、接口设计不合理；负残差大的任务，则可能因为训练数据熟悉、模板化、捷径存在而显得异常容易。这个诊断视角比单纯做一个回归器更有用。","experiments":"数据规模是这篇论文最扎实的部分。主分析子集包含 17 个 benchmark、5230 个任务、497 个 agent 配置，这些配置来自 216 个模型和 90 个 scaffold；总语料库包含 415,470 条 agent-task outcome。任务类型覆盖 coding/SWE、function calling、web navigation、computer use、terminal、math、ML、cybersecurity 和 general-purpose suites，时间跨度从 2010 到 2026。每个 agent-task 对平均约 1.2 次 trial。\n\n结果上，作者给出的核心数字是：在 K-fold 评估下，组合特征的 task difficulty 预测达到 Spearman ρ=0.399，且相对 baseline 的提升有统计显著性（p<0.001）；在 out-of-distribution 设置下，ρ=0.225。单一特征族里，entropy 最强。论文还指出 response AUC 可能偏乐观，即便 task difficulty 排序学得不好，AUC 仍然可以很高，因此 rank-based 指标更可信。更有信息量的是残差分析案例：一些任务被模型预测为不难，但真实 IRT 难度很高，最后发现是环境不可完成或 evaluator 失灵；另一些任务文本上看似复杂，实际却因数据熟悉或 shortcut 而容易。这说明 difficulty predictor 不只是一个打分器，还能帮助发现 benchmark 设计缺陷。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看数据与目标定义，也就是 17 个 benchmark 如何统一成 agent×task 响应矩阵，以及为什么作者选 1PL IRT 而不是直接用平均成功率。这里决定了整篇论文是否站得住。然后看评估部分，重点理解作者为什么批评 response AUC，并把 task-level rank correlation 作为主指标。接着看 feature ablation，尤其是 entropy 为什么在单特征里表现最好，以及组合特征带来了什么增益。最后看 residual diagnostics 的案例分析，这部分最接近实际使用场景：它告诉你难度预测器能否帮助发现坏任务、脏 verifier 和 shortcut。预计 25–35 分钟可以读完主线；如果只想抓结论，看 §3 的 IRT 建模、§4/§5 using only task descriptions and static metadata, instead of running expensive agent-environment interactions? The target is not model-specific uncertainty calibration, but a benchmark-level notion of latent task difficulty that transfers across agent configurations and benchmark families. This matters because long-horizon agent benchmarks are becoming costly to validate, and without ex ante difficulty estimates it is hard to build calibrated evaluations, progressive curricula, or efficient task filters.","importance":"Its relevance to pretraining is indirect but real. Difficulty estimation can support curriculum construction, synthetic task selection, benchmark curation, and contamination analysis. The paper makes three useful points. First, it turns task difficulty from an informal intuition into an estimable latent variable that can be ranked across tasks. Second, it shows that response-level AUC can be misleadingly optimistic: a predictor may separate easy and hard responses without learning the actual ordering of task difficulty. Third, it releases a large multi-benchmark corpus and uses IRT-derived difficulty targets, which should be useful for future work on agent scaling, benchmark design, and difficulty-aware sampling. error","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-06T09:41:41Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05806.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 next-token prediction 在 teacher forcing 下的一个老问题：训练时总看到真前缀，推理时却要吃自己生成的前缀，长程生成和规划会出现误差累积。已有两条路各有短板：MTP 让模型多看几个未来 token，但 horizon 仍短；NextLat 在 latent space 做自监督一步预测，能改善局部动态一致性，但多步 rollout 仍会快速漂移。HiLP 要解决的就是：如何在不改变推理时主干架构、也不引入推理额外开销的前提下，把更长时间尺度的预测结构直接压进预训练表示里。","importance":"这是一个值得精读的 pretrain 方向工作，因为它不是简单给 NTP 叠一个 auxiliary loss，而是明确针对“长程表征是否能在预训练阶段被塑形”这个问题提出层级 latent 方案。贡献有两层。第一，它把 NextLat 从单尺度一步 latent dynamics 扩展到多尺度预测，试图减少 latent rollout 的 compounding error。第二，它坚持 training-time only 的设计：层级模块和组合头在推理时都可以移除，保留标准 NTP 解码路径。这一点很关键，因为很多替代目标在研究上有意思，但一旦推理路径变复杂，就很难进入大规模 recipe。HiLP 至少在设计上兼顾了研究价值和落地可能性。对预训练研究员来说，更重要的启发是：如果长程 reasoning / planning 的一部分瓶颈来自表示空间缺少稳定的 coarse-grained predictive state，那么单纯扩大模型和数据未必是唯一方向，训练目标本身可以更主动地塑造多尺度状态。","technical_approach":"$f","experiments":"实验规模在研究型论文里算扎实：1B 参数模型，在 100B tokens 上预训练，使用 8 张 NVIDIA B200。评测分两类。第一类是下游零样本能力，用 LM Evaluation Harness 跑 HumanEval，以及用 DataComp for LLMs 中的 symbolic 和 multi-step benchmarks，比较对象包括标准 NTP、MTP、NextLat。论文给出的主结论是 HiLP 在需要更长程推理和规划的任务上优于这些基线。第二类是 speculative decoding 效率，在 held-out code 和 Nemotron-Climbmix 验证集上，报告每次 drafting step 被 verifier 接受的平均 token 数，以及 K=1..4 各位置的 draft-verifier argmax agreement。HiLP 在这些指标上也更好，说明它学到的 latent dynamics 不只是离线表征更平滑，也更适合做多 token 预测和校验一致性。最有信息量的分析来自 rollout horizon 实验：作者画了 latent prediction cross-entropy 随 rollout 步数到 8 的曲线。结果显示，HiLP 基本保住了 NextLat 的近程误差水平，但在更远 horizon 上 future prediction error 更低。这正对应论文的核心主张：层级 abstract latent 缓解了多步 latent rollout 的误差累积。全文片段没有给出表格中的完整数值，因此无法逐项复述具体提升百分点；但参数规模、训练 token 数、评测任务和趋势结论是明确的。","reading_guide":"建议按“问题—机制—证据”三步读。先看 Introduction，确认作者不是在替代 AR，而是在给 AR 预训练加一个多尺度 latent 预测信号。然后重点读 Methodology，尤其是 Fig.1、五项损失的定义、SWA 的作用、以及 combined NTP 路径里的 stop-gradient 设计。这部分是论文真正的新意，不只是多加一层 latent。实验部分优先看 Tab.1、Fig.2 和讨论里关于 rollout horizon 到 8 步的误差曲线。读的时候要问两个问题：一，HiLP 的收益主要来自更强的 auxiliary supervision，还是来自层级时间抽象本身；二，training-time only 的 apparatus 是否真的没有把收益偷偷建立在更复杂的 inference path 上。若时间有限，30 分钟可以抓住方法与主结果；若要判断是否值得复现，建议把损失权重、窗口大小 k、以及 speculative decoding 设置一并看清。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a core weakness of standard next-token prediction under teacher forcing: the model is trained on gold prefixes but must condition on its own generations at inference time, which leads to compounding errors over long horizons. Existing fixes are partial. Multi-token prediction extends the horizon only a little, while NextLat adds latent self-prediction but still rolls out one step at a time and accumulates error. HiLP asks whether a language model can be pretrained to encode longer-range predictive structure through a hierarchical latent objective, without changing the inference-time architecture.","importance":"This is a meaningful pretraining paper because it does more than attach another auxiliary loss to NTP. It makes a specific claim about representation shaping: long-horizon reasoning and planning may benefit from a latent state space that carries stable coarse-grained predictive information, not just token-local transition consistency. The contribution is also practical in an important way. The hierarchical machinery is used only during training and can be removed entirely at inference time, so the method preserves the standard autoregressive decoding path. That makes it much easier to think about as a recipe-level intervention rather than a separate model family.","technical_approach":"$10","experiments":"$11","reading_guide":"Read it in three passes. First, the introduction: the key point is that HiLP is not replacing autoregressive language modeling, but augmenting it with a multi-scale latent prediction signal. Second, spend time on the methodology, especially Fig. 1, the five-loss objective, the role of sliding-window attention, and the stop-gradient design in the combined-NTP path. That is where the real novelty sits. Third, go to the main results and the rollout-horizon analysis. The failure is not mainly that the model has stronger EOS beliefs under pure decoding; EOS pressure is nearly decoder-invariant. The failure arises because the sampler is allowed to act on those beliefs at distant positions. This matters for decoding, RL exploration, CoT supervision, and pretraining data design for masked diffusion LMs. Any-order generation is not automatically compatible with multi-step reasoning; commitment order needs to be treated as a controlled variable.","technical_approach":"The work instruments the denoising loop of LLaDA-8B-Instruct. The first is answer-only collapse: the model emits a bare answer followed by padding, skipping the reasoning region. The second is answer-first backfill: the final answer is fixed early, and the derivation is filled in later around an already committed conclusion. The paper then compares pure decoding with semi-autoregressive or block-wise decoding and shows that EOS pressure is almost the same across decoders, while EOS commitment differs by more than a factor of two. The intervention is window-restricted decoding: only positions within a window of size w after the left-most masked position are eligible for commitment. w=∞ recovers pure decoding, and w=1 approximates left-to-right generation. The method changes only eligibility, not logits or model weights.","experiments":"$12","reading_guide":"This is worth a careful read. As a result, silent memory corruption can pass through structurally valid caches and only become visible as downstream task degradation.","importance":"The paper addresses an underappreciated reliability problem that grows as models move beyond standard dense attention: heterogeneous attention memories have fragmented failure modes, and ad hoc checks no longer suffice. Its direct relevance is strongest for long-context, compressed-cache, sparse-attention, and recurrent/hybrid serving. For pretraining researchers, the main value is indirect but real: it makes explicit the interface between architecture design and deployment-time error propagation, showing exactly how compression, selection, and reuse of internal state can turn into user-visible quality loss.","technical_approach":"Any attention memory is decomposed into three operators: update, select, and read. Each stage exposes a local contract e_out ≤ a·e_in + b with failure budget δ, with the key requirement that a, b, and δ be computable from quantities actually available at runtime rather than from an uncompressed reference. Local contracts compose by affine composition plus a union bound into a request-level risk ledger. Every number is labeled by proof strength: certified if a sound upper bound exists and is not saturated, partially certified if the bound holds only on a decidable subset, and empirical if it is an observation without a bound. Composition inherits the weakest tier, so a chain with any empirical stage cannot be reported as end-to-end certified. The framework also enforces metric typing: contracts with different error calibers, such as ℓ2 versus max aggregation, do not compose unless an explicit bridge is provided. This check caught a real bug in an early version of the authors’ own chain.","experiments":"The framework is instantiated on six model configurations across five architecture families, covering dense KV caches, latent caches, learned sparse selectors, and recurrent states. It requires","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-06T08:33:13Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05747.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是一般的视频问答，而是 VLM 在连续第一视角视频中能否形成“全局空间表征”。更具体地说，模型是否能把沿轨迹看到的局部观察整合成一致的场景地图，并在一个从未出现在视频轨迹上的新视角下，回答“我在哪里、目标在哪里、场景整体长什么样”这三类问题。作者认为现有空间 benchmark 大多停留在单帧或少量视角，很多题目其实可以靠局部线索或单图感知完成，无法真正测到跨时间、跨视角、带全局坐标约束的空间推理能力。GST-Bench 的核心设计就是把这些捷径尽量堵住：目标物体在 query 视角中不可见，query 视角不在原视频轨迹上，答案采用距离和角度等精确数值而不是粗粒度左右前后判断，并显式引入 top-down 图像来测试局部观察到全局地图的映射能力。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它把“视频理解”里常被混在一起的局部感知、短程时序建模、全局空间整合拆开了，给出了一个更接近 embodied pretraining 需求的评测面。第二，它揭示了当前强 VLM 的失败模式并不统一：闭源大模型在把任务退化成局部可见后能大幅提升，说明主要短板在跨帧空间整合；不少开源模型即便在局部版本上也提不起来，说明感知和推理两端都弱。这种诊断对后续数据设计、架构设计、视频 tokenization 和训练目标都很有用。第三，作者不只做 benchmark，还给出同管线生成的 GST-Train，并展示定向训练能把 Qwen3-VL-8B 从 25.89 拉到 53.52，超过零样本闭源模型。这说明全局空间能力并非纯靠更大模型自然涌现，训练数据分布和任务构造本身就是关键变量。对做原生多模态预训练的人，这比单纯 leaderboard 更有信息量。","technical_approach":"方法上有三个关键点。其一是数据生成管线。作者基于 OmniGibson 和 BEHAVIOR-1K 的 50 个室内场景，自动采样多样化相机轨迹，并额外采样不在轨迹上的 query viewpoint。系统记录对象和视角的 3D 坐标，再投影到全局 top-down 图中，自动构造问答对。这里最重要的过滤规则是：所有 object localization 相关样本都要求目标在 query 视角不可见，从源头上避免单帧解题。其二是任务 taxonomy。围绕 embodied agent 的三个基本问题——self localization、object localization、scene structure understanding——组织成 12 个子任务。论文强调这些任务不是简单换问法，而是分别考察局部到全局映射、跨视角目标定位、全局布局恢复等不同能力。其三是诊断性对照设计。作者构造了 local variants，把目标改成在 query 视角可见，使任务更接近单图感知，从而把“看不见所以不会答”和“看得见但不会整合”区分开。这个设计很关键，因为它让 benchmark 不只是排名工具，也能定位瓶颈。\n\n从建模角度看，这篇工作本身不是提出新 VLM 架构，而是提出一种更严格的能力定义和数据构造方式。它隐含的研究判断是：如果训练目标没有迫使模型维护显式或隐式的全局空间状态，单靠通用图文视频语料，模型很容易学到局部相关性而不是可迁移的空间表征。top-down 图像的引入也很有意思，它相当于给“世界模型是否形成”提供了一个可操作的外部探针，比只问自然语言问题更接近结构化评估。","experiments":"实验部分最有信息量的是三组结果。第一组是主 benchmark 评测。作者评测了 22 个 VLM，覆盖开源、闭源以及偏 embodied 的模型。最强零样本模型 Gemini-3-Pro 只有 42.68，而人类是 79.08，差距很大。开源模型整体更低，最强的 InternVL3.5-38B 也只在 30 分左右，很多模型接近随机水平。这个结果说明当前视频 VLM 的“会看视频”并不等于“能建立全局空间地图”。第二组是 local variant 对照。闭源模型在局部版本上最高能提升 39 分，说明其单图感知和局部空间判断并不差，主要卡在跨帧整合和 novel viewpoint 推断；开源模型提升不稳定，说明问题不只是 memory 或 temporal aggregation，也包括基础视觉感知和空间 grounding。第三组是 GST-Train 微调。作者用同一生成管线构建训练集，来源包括 BEHAVIOR-1K、ArtVIP、HyperSim 和内部模拟场景。Qwen3-VL-8B 在 GST-Train 上微调后，GST-Bench 分数从 25.89 提升到 53.52，提升 27.63 分，并超过所有零样本闭源模型。这个幅度说明 benchmark 与训练数据分布高度对齐，同时也说明全局空间推理是可学的，不是单纯靠更大参数规模才能得到。\n\n从消融和分析角度，最值得注意的是作者对“局部可见/不可见”的严格控制，以及 query viewpoint 脱离原轨迹这一设置。它显著提高了任务难度，也更接近机器人探索后从新位置做判断的场景。另一个重要细节是答案采用精确数值指标，这避免了很多 benchmark 常见的模糊评分问题，使得误差分析更细。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction 里对现有空间 benchmark 缺陷的三点批评，再看任务定义和数据构造部分，重点确认作者是如何保证题目不能被单帧或局部启发式解掉的。真正的核心不在模型列表，而在 sample filtering、novel viewpoint 采样、top-down 映射和 12 个子任务的划分逻辑。\n\n精读时优先看三类图表：一是 benchmark taxonomy 和数据生成流程图，理解每类题到底测什么；二是主结果表，尤其是 human、闭源、开源、embodied 模型之间的差距；三是 local variant 与 GST-Train 微调结果，这两部分最能说明失败模式和可改进方向。若从预训练角度阅读，值得深挖的问题有三个：第一，现有视频预训练目标为什么没有逼出稳定的全局空间状态；第二，top-down supervision 是否可以转化为预训练中的辅助目标；第三，模拟数据带来的收益能否迁移到真实世界视频。阅读时间大约 45–60 分钟，若要做相关研究，数据构造细节和附录中的过滤规则需要仔细看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode of current VLMs: they may parse local visual content from videos, yet still fail to build a globally consistent spatial representation over time. The benchmark asks whether a model can integrate egocentric observations along a trajectory, infer spatial relations from a novel viewpoint that never appears in the input video, and align local observations with an explicit top-down scene representation. This is different from standard video QA. The authors deliberately remove shortcuts: the queried target is invisible from the query view, the query camera is sampled off-trajectory, and answers are evaluated with precise numerical quantities such as distance and angle rather than coarse left/right judgments.","importance":"$13","technical_approach":"$14","experiments":"$15","reading_guide":"Start with the benchmark motivation and the three limitations the authors identify in prior spatial benchmarks. Then read the dataset construction section carefully, especially the filtering rules that prevent single-frame shortcuts. The benchmark is only convincing if those controls are sound.\n\nThe most important figures and tables are: the task taxonomy, the data-generation pipeline, the main model comparison table, the local-vs-global diagnostic results, and the GST-Train fine-tuning results. For a pretraining-oriented reader, three questions are worth keeping in mind while reading: why current video pretraining objectives fail to induce stable global spatial representations; whether top-down alignment could be turned into an auxiliary pretraining objective; and how much of the gain from simulation-based data might transfer to real-world video. 代码库，但必须遵守固定执行接口和不可变路径约束。最终它提名一个候选 harness，在搜索期间不可访问的 test split 上打分，指标是相对 seed harness 的 normalized gain。协议强调三点：开发、验证、测试拆分固定且不重叠；held-out test 在搜索期间不可见，避免优化器拟合可见分数；trusted execution environment design 下，共享 harness 内不同 optimizer model 的差异，平均大于 shared harness 与 native harness 的差异；native harness 没有稳定优势。可获得的提升强烈依赖任务和 seed regime。OfficeQA 的 release-level 实验显示该 benchmark harness under a fixed budget. The harness includes prompts, tools, control flow, memory, and orchestration code. The core difficulty is not ordinary coding, but making reliable system-level improvements when evaluation is expensive, stochastic, and only partially observed.","importance":"This is mainly a skim-worthy agent evaluation paper rather than a pretraining method paper. Its relevance is in the interface between model capability and system capability. As LLM deployments depend heavily on harnesses, evaluating only the base model or the chat model misses an important layer. The benchmark contributes a controlled protocol: fixed target model and environment, held-out final evaluation, strict budget enforcement, and full candidate auditing. This helps separate optimizer-model ability from coding-harness effects and protocol noise.","technical_approach":"HarnessOpt-Bench formulates harness optimization as constrained stochastic program optimization. An optimizer, consisting of an LLM plus a coding harness, receives a seed harness, graded development and validation feedback, and a limited target-evaluation budget. It may edit the executable harness codebase while respecting a fixed interface and immutable paths. The final submitted candidate is evaluated on an inaccessible test split, using normalized gain over the seed harness as the score. The protocol relies on fixed non-overlapping dev/validation/test splits, graded disclosure, and a trusted execution environment that enforces access control, meters target evaluations and resources, isolates held-out state, and versions all candidates. This differs from prior demonstrations where each method often used its own target agent, seed, budget, and scoring protocol.","experiments":"The suite contains 4 downstream tasks designed to have costly and noisy evaluation, including fixed-corpus research and terminal-use settings. The authors evaluate 5 frontier optimizer models under a shared coding harness and under their respective native harnesses, producing 111 scored optimizer runs. They also evaluate 2 additional coding harnesses on one task. Under this paired setup, differences among optimizer models within the shared harness are larger on average than differences between shared and native harnesses. Native harnesses do not provide a consistent advantage. Achievable gains vary strongly by task and seed regime. On OfficeQA, release-level experiments show that the benchmark can resolve variation across successive optimizer-model releases. Instrumented trajectories suggest that broader interventions correlate with higher held-out gain, while detailed failure-trace inspection is rare and not positively associated with gain.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Each prompt is run three times per condition, and all responses are collected within a one-week window to minimize temporal drift. games，尤其是扑克这类高噪声环境中处理，并进一步考虑一个现实难点：如果评测时还想在线更新 value model 来做方差缩减，怎样避免当前对局的信息泄漏到自己的校正项里，导致统计保证失效。","importance":"它对 LLM 基座研究不是直接的预训练方法贡献，而是偏评测基础设施。启发点在于：当 agent 在同一停止时刻重算，几乎能推翻这些错误结论。进一步地，当 AsympCS 按自己的 stopping rule 继续运行时，到 5,000 hands 时对注入的 0.1 和 0.2 BB 效应分别能在 81% 和 100% 的条目中检测出来。最后，论文还强调可复核性：只要公开 corrected outcomes 到停止时刻、停止规则、目标、置信水平、bound 和 CS The setting here is especially challenging because the games are imperfect-information games, where variance is high, and because the evaluator may want to improve its value model online during evaluation. verifier. Its central observation is that real and hallucinated object tokens are separable in hidden representations, but this separability is largely weakened after the LM head maps them into vocabulary probabilities.","importance":"The work is useful for studying the gap between internal representations and exposed model outputs. LVLM hidden states already encode some truthfulness or uncertainty signal, but standard next-token training does not force the LM head to make that signal readable. TruthLens uses a special token as a token-level truthfulness channel. This connects to verifier design, reward modeling, self-confidence calibration, and hallucination control. It also suggests that pretraining or post-training objectives can include structured readout signals instead of relying only on free-form generation.","technical_approach":"The method starts with a diagnostic comparison. Token-level negative log-likelihood is used to inspect LM-head confidence, while LDA is applied to hidden states to measure separability between real and hallucinated object tokens. The finding is that hidden states are more discriminative than output distributions. TruthLens then repurposes a rarely used special token as a reference token. At the prediction position of each object token, the method reads the log-probability assigned to this special token by the LM head and subtracts a predefined constant to obtain a truthfulness score. During training, multiple captions are sampled for each image, object tokens are labeled as real or hallucinated, and an MSE loss pushes real-token scores toward 1 and hallucinated-token scores toward 0. A divergence constraint keeps the updated model close to the original generation behavior. At inference time, the score is read from the existing LM head distribution, so no auxiliary model or extra forward pass is required.","experiments":"The paper evaluates the method across multiple LVLMs and object hallucination benchmarks, reporting consistent gains over existing detection approaches. A notable result is that the training annotations cover only a limited set of object categories, yet the learned signal transfers to benchmarks with much larger label spaces. This suggests the method is not merely memorizing category labels, but learning a token-level groundedness readout. The supplied content does not provide exact numbers, model names, or metric tables, so the magnitude of the gains should be verified in the experimental section. Important ablations include the choice of special token, the predefined constant and threshold μ, the MSE supervision, the divergence constraint, layer-wise hidden-state separability, and cross-model generalization.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes.
